URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT
Madam Kama Marg, Hutatma Rajguru Chowk,
Mantralaya, Mumbai-400 032.
Date : 29th April 2026
The Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966
NOTIFICATION
No. TPS-1824/100/CR-16/2025/UD-13.––Whereas, under the provisions of Section 151 of the
Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning Act, 1966 (Maharashtra Act No. XXXVII of 1966) (hereinafter
referred to as “the said Act”) and in exercise of the powers conferred under sub-section (2) of Section
68 of the said Act regarding the approval of the Draft Town Planning Scheme, the Metropolitan
Commissioner, Pune Metropolitan Region Development Authority, has sanctioned the Draft Town Planning
Scheme Mhalunge-Maan No. 1 (First Variation) (hereinafter referred to as “the said Draft Scheme”) through
Notification No. Niyojan/Mhalunge-Maan No. 1/First varied/752/2022, dated 30th December 2022, issued
by the Pune Metropolitan Region Development Authority and the said Notification has been published in
the official Gazette, Part One-Pune Divisional Supplement, dated 31st December 2022 ;
and whereas, the Government of Maharashtra in Urban Development Department vide Notification
No. TPS-1820/150/CR.-11/2020/Arbitrator/UD-13, dated 20th September 2022 issued under sub-section
(1) of Section 72 of the said Act, appointed the Arbitrator for the said Draft Scheme (hereinafter referred
to as “the said Arbitrator”) ;
and whereas, in accordance with the amended provisions of Section 148-A of the said Act, vide the
Maharashtra Regional and Town Planning (Amendment) Act, 2020 (Maharashtra Act No. XIX of 2020), in
computing the period, in relation to any Development Plan, Regional Plan or Scheme under the provisions
of Chapter II, III, IV and V of the said Act, the period of periods during which any action could not be
completed under the said chapters, due to enforcement of any Guidelines or lockdown measures by the
Government of India or the State Government, as the case may be, to prevent the spread of any pandemic
or epidemic or disaster situation arising in the Country or State shall be excluded ;
and whereas, after excluding such period, the said Arbitrator after following the prescribed legal
procedure and finalising the said Draft Scheme, has submitted Mhalunge-Maan Town Planning Scheme
No.1 (First Variation) to the Government for sanction under sub-section (5) of Section 72 of the said Act,
vide letter dated 3rd January 2024 (hereinafter referred to as “the said Preliminary Scheme”) ;
and whereas, after making necessary enquiries and after consulting the Director of Town Planning,
Maharashtra State, Pune, the Government of Maharashtra is of opinion that it is necessary to sanction
the said Preliminary Scheme with some changes :–
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) of Section 86 of the said Act
the State Government hereby, sanctions Mhalunge-Maan Town Planning Scheme No. 1 (First Variation)
with some changes as described in the Schedule-I (regarding Final Plots) and Schedule-II (regarding
Development Control And Promotion And Regulation of Town Planning Scheme No. 2) appended
hereto :–
(1) The said sanctioned Mhalunge-Maan No.1 (First Variation) Preliminary Town Planning Scheme
shall come into force after one month from the date of publication of Notification in the Official
Gazette, as per Section 86 (2) (b) of the said Act.
(2) This Notification of sanctioning the Mhalunge-Maan No. 1 (First Variation) Preliminary Town
Planning Scheme, shall be available for inspection of Public during working hours on all working
days for a period of one month in the following offices.
i) The Metropolitan Commissioner, Pune Metropolitan Region Development Authority, Pune.
ii) The Arbitrator, Town Planning Scheme, Mhalunge-Maan No. 1 (First Variation), Pune Metropolitan
Region Development Authority, Akurdi, Pune.
(3) The present Arbitrator shall be deemed to have been appointed as with retrospective effect for
Town Planning Scheme Mhalunge-Man No. 1 (First Variation) as per Government Notification No.
TPS-1820/150/C.R.11/2020/Arbitration/UD-13, dated 20th September 2022, issued under Section
72 (2) of the said Act.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, Jihe
Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, Jihe
Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.
Schedule – A

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
of Water User Associations (WUAs) on Volumetric basis and as per Administrative Convenience under
section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005. I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer,
Jihe Kathapur L.I. Division, Satara hereby delineate the areas of Operation of following WUA’s direct that
the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Engineer Amol Panditrao Nikam, Executive Engineer, also hereby, declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the Appropriate Authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management of Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Satara
Irrigation Project Circle, Satara within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government gazette.

MMISF ACT 2005
(See Sections 5.0, 6.0, 7.7 and Rule 3.00)
NOTIFICATION NO - III
Water User Associations (WUAs) on volumetric basis and as per administrative convenience under section
5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act, 2005, I Dhananjay Jivandhar Kondekar , Executive Engineer, Ujani
Canal Division No. 8 Solapur, hereby delineate the areas of operation of following WUA’s direct that the
certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and / or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be
displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchyat, Tahsil Office, Irrigation Section,
Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Dhananjay Jivandhar Kondekar, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that, no water shall be
supplied by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of
water through Water Users Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Maharashtra Management Irrigation System by Farmers Act-2005.
Any person affected by this notification or part thereof may, within 30 days from the date of
Publication in the Maharashtra Govt. Gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Bhima Canal
Circle, Solapur within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.
